None appears for the applicants. Heard Mr. B.J. Talukdar, learned counsel for the writ
petitioners. Also heard Ms. D.D. Barman, learned Govt. Advocate, Assam, Mr. R. Borpujari,
learned standing counsel, Finance Department as well as Mr. P. Kakati, learned standing
counsel, Water Resource Department appearing for the official respondents.
The applicants in all these cases are seeking impleadment in the connected writ petition, inter

Page No.# 477/478
alia, on the ground that they are also similarly situated as the writ petitioners and therefore,
are entitled to similar benefits as may be granted to the writ petitioners in this case.
Mr. Talukdar, learned counsel appearing for the writ petitioners submits that he does not
dispute the claim of the applicants and therefore, would have no objection if the prayer for
impleadment made by them is allowed by this Court.
Ms. D.D. Barman, learned Govt. Advocate, Assam, Mr. R. Borpujari, learned standing counsel,
Finance Department and Mr. P, Kakati, learned standing counsel, Water Resource Department
have also submitted in tandem that they are not opposing the prayer for impleadment made
by the applicants.
After hearing the submission of learned counsel for the parties and on perusal of the
statement made in the respective applications, I am of the considered opinion that the
applicants are interested in the subject matter of controversy involved in the writ petition and
therefore, are required to be heard before any final order is passed in the writ petition.
What relief, if any, can ultimately be granted to the applicants by this Court is an entirely
different matter and can be gone into at an appropriate stage after hearing the submissions
of learned counsel for the parties.
In view of the above, although the applicants counsel is absent today, I am of the view that
all these I.A.s can be disposed of by allowing the prayer for impleadment made by the
applicants therein.
Ordered accordingly.
The applicants in all these I.A.s shall stand impleaded as respondents in W.P.(C) No.
3900/2017.

Page No.# 478/478
Registry to make necessary correction in the cause title and thereafter, list the writ petition
for necessary order(s) after reflecting the names of learned counsel for all the parties in the
cause list.
I.A. stands disposed of accordingly.
